# Agents · F · part 6

954 entries, fp-judge to frontend-developer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **fp-judge**: Rule on one analyzer finding a builder believes is a false positive, seeing only the case file and the repository — never the builder's reasoning. Returns REAL with a concrete fix, or FALSE_POSITIVE with a check that could have shown the finding was real. Sits twice on every false-positive panel, each time on a different model. Dispatched by `vp run adjudicate:panel`. ([luciocabrera/lcabrera-stack](https://github.com/luciocabrera/lcabrera-stack/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fp-judge.md))
- **fp-pattern-investigator**: Investigate a single FP pattern to determine if it's truly a false positive or might be a real threat. Designed to be spawned in parallel (one instance per pattern) by the fp-pattern-finder skill. Returns structured verdict with reasoning. ([refractionPOINT/lc-ai](https://github.com/refractionPOINT/lc-ai/blob/HEAD/marketplace/plugins/lc-advanced-skills/agents/fp-pattern-investigator.md) · ★31)
- **fp-performance-reviewer**: Reviews code for performance issues like memory leaks, slow queries, unnecessary computation, bundle size, and runtime bottlenecks. Use proactively after changes to hot paths, data processing, or API endpoints. ([findexu/finpack-claude](https://github.com/findexu/finpack-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-performance-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **fp-plan-reviewer**: Reviews a plan or expedition steps for executable completeness, risk, gaps, and scope drift before work begins. Returns a verdict (READY | REVISE) so a planning loop can terminate. Used by /counsel-plan and /embark. NOT a code reviewer (that is fp-code-reviewer). ([findexu/finpack-claude](https://github.com/findexu/finpack-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-plan-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **fp-reduction**: Applies the six-stage FP-reduction rubric to a unified-finding stream, producing a disposition register. Consumes findings + RECON (+ CPG when joern is available). ([bdfinst/agentic-dev-team](https://github.com/bdfinst/agentic-dev-team/blob/HEAD/plugins/security-assessment/agents/fp-reduction.md) · ★285)
- **fp-reduction**: Turn a raw unified-finding list into a disposition register the exec-report-generator can trust. Every input finding produces exactly one output entry — a `false_positive` verdict is still an entry. The audit trail matters as much as the report. ([bdfinst/agentic-dev-team](https://github.com/bdfinst/agentic-dev-team/blob/HEAD/plugins/security-assessment/docs/agents/fp-reduction.md) · ★285)
- **fp-researcher**: Investigates an unfamiliar domain, library, API or design question and returns a concise findings brief. Dispatched by /fp:propose to de-risk a proposal. Read-only — it never edits the project. ([saurabhhbansal/flightplan](https://github.com/saurabhhbansal/flightplan/blob/HEAD/plugins/fp/agents/fp-researcher.md))
- **fp-security-reviewer**: Reviews code changes for security vulnerabilities. Use for PR review, pre-deploy verification, or audit of recently changed files. ([findexu/finpack-claude](https://github.com/findexu/finpack-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-security-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **fp-structure-reviewer**: Council persona for /council reviews. Spawn only inside a council review workflow. Mark Seemann - functional architecture, impureim sandwich, dependency rejection. ([smykla-skalski/sai](https://github.com/smykla-skalski/sai/blob/HEAD/plugins/council/agents/fp-structure-reviewer.agent.md) · ★4)
- **fp-structure-reviewer**: Council persona for /council orchestrator. Spawn only inside a council review workflow. Mark Seemann - functional architecture, impureim sandwich, dependency rejection. ([smykla-skalski/sai](https://github.com/smykla-skalski/sai/blob/HEAD/claude/council/agents/fp-structure-reviewer.md) · ★4)
- **fp-swiftui-designer**: Designs and builds polished SwiftUI UI/UX for iPhone and iPad apps. Use when building or refining native Apple app screens, components, navigation, or adaptive iPad layouts. Follows Apple Human Interface Guidelines, builds adaptive (size-class aware) layouts, and produces production-ready SwiftUI code. ([findexu/finpack-claude](https://github.com/findexu/finpack-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/fp-swiftui-designer.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **fpa-analyst**: Use for FP&A work — budgeting, rolling forecasts, KPI commentary, variance walks, P&L-level scenario modeling, and headcount/opex planning. Spawn for budget season, variance commentary, KPI-pack assembly, and board-pack inserts. NOT for three-statement model build or close mechanics (controller). ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/finance/agents/fpa-analyst.md) · ★7)
- **fpa-analyst**: FP&A specialist for budgeting, financial forecasting, variance analysis, and driver-based planning. Use for financial planning, budgets, or forecasting tasks. ([brainbytes-dev/everything-claude-finance](https://github.com/brainbytes-dev/everything-claude-finance/blob/HEAD/agents/fpa-analyst.md) · ★6)
- **fpa-analyst**: Activate when the company needs durable ownership over driver-based modeling, variance analysis, scenario planning, and forecast hygiene. FP&A Analyst converts recurring work in this lane into explicit artifacts, operating rules, and measurable decisions. ([einstenrodriguesdev/drax-plugin-cc](https://github.com/einstenrodriguesdev/drax-plugin-cc/blob/HEAD/agents/fpa-analyst.md))
- **fpf-advisor**: EN: FPF thinking advisor that surfaces structured-reasoning suggestions for architecture, planning, and decision-making tasks. HOOK-triggered background agent — recommends /fpf-decompose, /fpf-evaluate, or /fpf-reason based on context. Does not activate for simple coding tasks or routine edits. RU: Советник FPF, предлагающий структурированное мышление для задач архитектуры, планирования и принятия решений. Фоновый агент через HOOK — рекомендует /fpf-decompose, /fpf-evaluate или /fpf-reason по контексту. Не активируется для простых задач кодирования или рутинных правок. Triggers: "architecture… ([ForgePlan/marketplace](https://github.com/ForgePlan/marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/fpf/agents/fpf-advisor.md) · ★3)
- **fpga-orchestrator**: Orchestrates FPGA prototyping — ASIC-to-FPGA RTL adaptation, partitioning, FPGA synthesis, hardware bring-up, and software validation. Invoke when porting an ASIC design to Xilinx or Intel FPGA for pre-silicon software development and hardware validation. ([hdl-tools/digital-chip-design-agents](https://github.com/hdl-tools/digital-chip-design-agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/fpga/agents/fpga-orchestrator.md) · ★205)
- **FPGA/ASIC 数字设计工程师**: FPGA 与 ASIC 数字前端设计专家——精通 Verilog/SystemVerilog、VHDL、Vivado/Quartus、AXI/AHB 总线、时序收敛、Zynq/Intel SoC FPGA、高层次综合（HLS）。 ([azusagasaku/--claude-config](https://github.com/azusagasaku/--claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/engineering-fpga-digital-design-engineer.md))
- **fpl-contract-checker**: Verifies that a frontend API call matches the backend controller that serves it — path, method, request DTO, response shape, error codes. Use before wiring a new endpoint into the UI, after changing a DTO, or when a response is undefined at runtime. Read-only; reports mismatches, does not fix them. ([Shreedhar73/fpl-orchestrator](https://github.com/Shreedhar73/fpl-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/plugins/fpl/agents/fpl-contract-checker.md))
- **fpl-data-analyst**: Read-only analyst over the project's Postgres and the official FPL API. Use for "which players have the best fixtures", "how has this player's price moved", "what does the data say about X" — questions answered by querying, not by editing. Returns findings with the query that produced them. Cannot write files or mutate data. ([Shreedhar73/fpl-orchestrator](https://github.com/Shreedhar73/fpl-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/plugins/fpl/agents/fpl-data-analyst.md))
- **fpp-diagnostician**: FPP-Forecast v2 paralel akışının SIRALI teşhis aşaması. Veri alımı + teşhis + deterministik aday seçimini scripts/fpp_ingest.py ve scripts/fpp_diagnose.py ile yapar; profile.json + candidates.json üretir. Orkestratör fan-out'tan ÖNCE bir kez çağırır. &lt;example&gt; Context: Orkestratör ham veri dosyasını hazırladı, paralel tahmin başlatacak. user: "input=satis.csv run_dir=.../fpp_run_satis için teşhis yap" assistant: "fpp-diagnostician agent'ını çağırıp profile.json + candidates.json üretiyorum." &lt;commentary&gt;Fan-out öncesi zorunlu, sıralı teşhis adımı.&lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([ERDEMRD/fpp-forecast](https://github.com/ERDEMRD/fpp-forecast/blob/HEAD/agents/fpp-diagnostician.md))
- **fpp-model-worker**: FPP-Forecast v2 paralel akışının PARALEL fan-out işçisi. TEK bir aday modeli scripts/fpp_fit.py --id &lt;id&gt; ile eğitir (train/test + CV + kalıntı + aralık + geri-dönüşüm). Soğuk başlar; durumu run_dir'den okur. Orkestratör her aday için BUNDAN BİR TANE, hepsini aynı anda başlatır. &lt;example&gt; Context: Teşhisçi candidates.json üretti; orkestratör fan-out ediyor. user: "run_dir=.../fpp_run_satis, id=AutoETS modelini eğit" assistant: "fpp-model-worker agent'ını AutoETS için çağırıyorum; diğer adaylar paralel işçilerde." &lt;commentary&gt;Her aday model ayrı, eşzamanlı işçide eğitilir.&lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/examp… ([ERDEMRD/fpp-forecast](https://github.com/ERDEMRD/fpp-forecast/blob/HEAD/agents/fpp-model-worker.md))
- **fpp-reducer**: FPP-Forecast v2 paralel akışının SIRALI birleştirme aşaması. scripts/fpp_select.py (kalıntı kapısı + MASE + basitlik ilkesi + kombinasyon) ve scripts/fpp_report.py (14 bölümlük notebook + Türkçe rapor) çalıştırır. Orkestratör tüm işçiler döndükten SONRA bir kez çağırır. &lt;example&gt; Context: Tüm fpp-model-worker'lar results/*.json yazdı. user: "run_dir=.../fpp_run_satis sonuçlarını birleştir, notebook + rapor üret" assistant: "fpp-reducer agent'ını çağırıp seçimi ve nihai çıktıları üretiyorum." &lt;commentary&gt;Fan-out sonrası tek seferlik birleştirme/seçim adımı.&lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([ERDEMRD/fpp-forecast](https://github.com/ERDEMRD/fpp-forecast/blob/HEAD/agents/fpp-reducer.md))
- **fr-analyst**: FR Analyst agent for converting user requirements into complete FR documentation packages. Use when a new feature, flow, or requirement is described by the user. - **fr-nfr-reviewer**: BMAD spec-compliance reviewer for SDLC stage 4 frontend work. Delegate to this agent when /fe-sdlc-review (or the /fe-sdlc orchestrator) needs the React change set verified against the planning specs bundle — it runs the FR/NFR gate script against specs/&lt;slug&gt;/, builds the per-requirement PASS/FAIL matrix covering every FR, every NFR, every pinned NFR catalog category, expanded quality dimension, system quality attribute, whole-codebase impact surface, manual-evidence item, GitHub completion gate, and CI check, frontend-flavored (Lighthouse perf budgets, accessibility, visual regression), and… ([VilnaCRM-Org/claude-plugins](https://github.com/VilnaCRM-Org/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/react-frontend-sdlc/agents/fr-nfr-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **fr-nfr-reviewer**: Use when a completed change needs independent FR/NFR acceptance and shipped-skill coverage review. Use qa-infrastructure-tester to execute E2E scenarios, security-reviewer for security findings, and evidence-and-coverage to calculate inventory metrics. ([VilnaCRM-Org/claude-plugins](https://github.com/VilnaCRM-Org/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/devops-sdlc/agents/fr-nfr-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **fr-nfr-reviewer**: BMAD spec-compliance reviewer for SDLC stage 4. Delegate to this agent when /sdlc-review (or the /sdlc orchestrator) needs the change set verified against the planning specs bundle — it runs the FR/NFR gate script against specs/&lt;slug&gt;/, builds the per-requirement PASS/FAIL matrix covering every FR, every NFR, and every quality dimension, and tracks the new-findings count per gate iteration so the review loop can detect convergence (zero new findings). Triggers — FR/NFR review, spec compliance check, requirement traceability, requirement matrix, review gate, fr-nfr-gate, zero new findings, BMA… ([VilnaCRM-Org/claude-plugins](https://github.com/VilnaCRM-Org/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/php-backend-sdlc/agents/fr-nfr-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **fr-phase-executor**: Implement ONE plan phase, serially, inside an already-active fr-isolation workspace, then return a structured result. Dispatch it INTO that workspace, never into a second worktree — fr's worktree already IS this agent's working copy, and a second one strands it on `main` with the spec and plan invisible and every Bash/Edit call denied. fr-goal dispatches each phase here so the phase's file reads, test output, and dead ends stay out of the orchestrator's context. NOT a general-purpose agent and NOT for parallel work — it operates on the shared feature branch one phase at a time (parallel phase… ([derio-net/super-fr](https://github.com/derio-net/super-fr/blob/HEAD/packages/fr/src/fr/agents/fr-phase-executor.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **fr-spec-reviewer**: Review ONE freshly written design spec, read-only, in a context separate from the one that wrote it, and return a structured list of findings. fr-goal dispatches it at `spec-review`, into the fr-isolation workspace the spec lives in — never into a second worktree, which would be cut from `main` where the feature branch's spec does not exist. It checks the spec against the operator's recorded decisions, against the codebase it names, and against itself; it tags every finding in scope or out of scope, and it writes nothing. NOT a general-purpose reviewer and NOT for code review — `review-phase`… ([derio-net/super-fr](https://github.com/derio-net/super-fr/blob/HEAD/packages/fr/src/fr/agents/fr-spec-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **fr-specialist**: Functional requirements specialist. Converts assigned needs from the orchestrator's generation_brief into atomic functional requirements written in EARS notation (choosing the correct pattern per requirement), each with a mandatory rationale, fit criterion, and Gherkin (Given/When/Then) acceptance criteria. Returns a draft_requirements object. ([Stormbreaker9000/groundwork](https://github.com/Stormbreaker9000/groundwork/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/fr-specialist.md) · ★1)
- **fr-to-ready**: Feature-request-to-dev-ready workflow. Takes a raw feature idea or existing FR through authoring, AC verification, and engineering handoff — running write-feature-request, verify-acceptance-criteria, and write-technical-brief in sequence. Use when you want a complete, dev-ready handoff package in one session. Trigger on: "run fr-to-ready", "make this FR dev-ready", "I need a complete handoff package for this feature", "get this feature ready for engineering", "full FR pipeline". ([felipecabargas/gambit](https://github.com/felipecabargas/gambit/blob/HEAD/agents/fr-to-ready.md) · ★2)
- **fr-worker**: Work ONE roomler FR card from its current state toward Ready-to-close — implement the next phase in its own worktree, get CI green, write the owed docs in the same PR, gather REAL field evidence, tick only the acceptance criteria that evidence supports, and park. Never closes an issue, never merges a PR, never claims an FR number. Invoked by the `autopilot` skill with a card id; read that skill first for the phases and the rails. ([gjovanov/roomler-ai](https://github.com/gjovanov/roomler-ai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fr-worker.md))

- **frame-picker**: Generates one pixel art frame in a single set_frame call (all pixels at once). Fastest but least iterative control. ([bijeebuss/claude-code-sprite-maker](https://github.com/bijeebuss/claude-code-sprite-maker/blob/HEAD/agents/frame-picker.md))
- **frame-processor**: Cheap deterministic agent for turning source video into web-optimized frame sequences with FFmpeg (via framify.mjs) and general file/asset wrangling. Use for the video-to-frames step and mechanical processing. No creative judgment needed. ([Grunte12/creative-studio](https://github.com/Grunte12/creative-studio/blob/HEAD/plugins/creative-studio/agents/frame-processor.md))
- **frame-researcher**: Investigates the questions a FRAME lead agent assigns - causes of defects, dependency and consumer behaviour, and comparisons between approaches - by inspecting code, callers, tests, and authoritative documentation, and returns findings with the evidence and source locations behind them. Use when a substantial investigation benefits from its own context, rather than when the lead could resolve the question directly for less work. Does not write production code. ([Zicrael/FRAME](https://github.com/Zicrael/FRAME/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md) · ★3)
- **frame-reviewer**: Read-only frame-layer adversary. Attacks the frame's problem statement, discovery, and open questions. Returns OK | GAPS. Never sees a diff. Single pass, no loop. ([limaagabriel/sherpa](https://github.com/limaagabriel/sherpa/blob/HEAD/agents/frame-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **frame-validator**: Independently assesses a FRAME lead agent's candidate - an implementation or a set of findings - against the original request, its constraints, and the behaviour that had to be preserved, then reports demonstrated defects, omitted required work, unsupported conclusions, and material verification gaps with their evidence.
